!strings.HasPrefix(n.Config().NetworkMode, \"container:\") {\n\t\t\tn.Config().ExtraHosts = extraHosts\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// certificateAuthoritySetup sets up the certificate authority parameters.\nfunc (c *CLab) certificateAuthoritySetup() error {\n\t// init the Cert storage and CA\n\tc.Cert.CertStorage = clabcert.NewLocalDirCertStorage(c.TopoPaths)\n\tc.Cert.CA = clabcert.NewCA()\n\n\ts := c.Config.Settings\n\n\t// Set defaults for the CA parameters\n\tkeySize := 2048\n\tvalidityDuration := time.Until(time.Now().AddDate(1, 0, 0)) // 1 year as default\n\tvar extCACert, extCAKey string\n\n\t// check that Settings.CertificateAuthority exists.\n\tif s != nil && s.CertificateAuthority != nil {\n\t\t// if ValidityDuration is set use the value\n\t\tif s.CertificateAuthority.ValidityDuration != 0 {\n\t\t\tvalidityDuration = s.CertificateAuthority.ValidityDuration\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// if KeyLength is set use the value\n\t\tif s.CertificateAuthority.KeySize != 0 {\n\t\t\tkeySize = s.CertificateAuthority.KeySize\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// if external CA cert and key are set, propagate to topopaths\n\t\textCACert = s.CertificateAuthority.Cert\n\t\textCAKey = s.CertificateAuthority.Key\n\t}\n\n\t// override external ca and key from env vars\n\tif v := os.Getenv(\"CLAB_CA_KEY_FILE\"); v != \"\" {\n\t\textCAKey = v\n\t}\n\n\tif v := os.Getenv(\"CLAB_CA_CERT_FILE\"); v != \"\" {\n\t\textCACert = v\n\t}\n\n\tif extCACert != \"\" && extCAKey != \"\" {\n\t\terr := c.TopoPaths.SetExternalCaFiles(extCACert, extCAKey)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// define the attributes used to generate the CA Cert\n\tcaCertInput := &clabcert.CACSRInput{\n\t\tCommonName:   c.Config.Name + \" lab CA\",\n\t\tCountry:      \"US\",\n\t\tExpiry:       validityDuration,\n\t\tOrganization: \"containerlab\",\n\t\tKeySize:      keySize,\n\t}\n\n\treturn c.LoadOrGenerateCA(caCertInput)\n}\n\n// createNodes schedules nodes creation and returns a waitgroup for all nodes\n// with the exec collection created from the exec config of each node.\n// The exec collection is returned to the caller to ensure that the execution log\n// is printed after the nodes are created.\n// Nodes interdependencies are created in this function.\nfunc (c *CLab) createNodes(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tmaxWorkers uint,\n\tskipPostDeploy bool,\n) (*sync.WaitGroup, *clabexec.ExecCollection, chan error, error) {\n\tfor _, node := range c.Nodes {\n\t\tc.dependencyManager.AddNode(node)\n\t}\n\n\t// nodes with static mgmt IP should be scheduled before the dynamic ones\n\terr := c.createStaticDynamicDependency()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, nil, err\n\t}\n\n\t// create user-defined node dependencies done with `wait-for` property of the deployment stage\n\terr = c.createWaitForDependency()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, nil, err\n\t}\n\n\t// make network namespace shared containers start in the right order\n\tc.createNamespaceSharingDependency()\n\n\t// Add possible additional dependencies here\n\n\t// make sure that there are no unresolvable dependencies, which would deadlock.\n\terr = c.dependencyManager.CheckAcyclicity()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, nil, err\n\t}\n\n\t// Buffered so workers never block reporting a failure (at most one failure path per node).\n\t// With zero nodes, nothing sends on this channel; an empty buffer is fine.\n\tnodeFailCh := make(chan error, len(c.Nodes))\n\n\t// start scheduling\n\tNodesWg, execCollection := c.scheduleNodes(ctx, int(maxWorkers), skipPostDeploy, nodeFailCh)\n\n\treturn NodesWg, execCollection, nodeFailCh, nil\n}\n\n// DeployNodes runs the node creation phase for the selected nodes.\nfunc (c *CLab) DeployNodes(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tnodeNames []string,\n\tmaxWorkers uint,\n) error {\n\treturn c.deployNodes(ctx, nodeNames, maxWorkers, nil)\n}\n\n// deployApplyNodes schedules starts and creates together: a new sidecar can\n// depend on a stopped target, and a stopped node can depend on a new target.\nfunc (c *CLab) deployApplyNodes(ctx context.Context, plan *applyPlan, maxWorkers uint) error {\n\tnodeNames := sortedStringSet(\n\t\tunionStringSets(plan.addedNodeSet, plan.recreatedNodeSet, plan.startNodeSet),\n\t)\n\treturn c.deployNodes(ctx, nodeNames, maxWorkers, plan.startNodeSet)\n}\n\nfunc (c *CLab) deployNodes(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tnodeNames []string,\n\tmaxWorkers uint,\n\tstartNodeSet map[string]struct{},\n) error {\n\tif len(nodeNames) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tif maxWorkers == 0 || int(maxWorkers) > len(nodeNames) {\n\t\tmaxWorkers = uint(len(nodeNames))\n\t}\n\tif _, err := c.networkModeNodeOrder(nodeNames); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tinput := make(chan string)\n\terrCh := make(chan error, len(nodeNames))\n\tcompleted := make(map[string]*nodeDeployCompletion, len(nodeNames))\n\tfor _, name := range nodeNames {\n\t\tcompleted[name] = &nodeDeployCompletion{done: make(chan struct{})}\n\t}\n\tfinish := func(name string, err error) {\n\t\tcompleted[name].err = err\n\t\tclose(completed[name].done)\n\t\terrCh <- err\n\t}\n\n\tfor range maxWorkers {\n\t\tgo func() {\n\t\t\tfor nodeName := range input {\n\t\t\t\tif _, start := startNodeSet[nodeName]; start {\n\t\t\t\t\tlog.Info(\"Starting stopped node\", \"node\", nodeName)\n\t\t\t\t\terr := c.Nodes[nodeName].Start(ctx)\n\t\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\t\terr = fmt.Errorf(\"failed starting node %q: %w\", nodeName, err)\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tfinish(nodeName, err)\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tlog.Info(\"Creating node\", \"node\", nodeName)\n\t\t\t\tfinish(nodeName, c.deployNode(ctx, c.Nodes[nodeName]))\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}()\n\t}\n\n\t// Wait outside the worker pool. Targets in this operation signal completion\n\t// directly, avoiding runtime polling and propagating failures to dependents.\n\tvar feedWg sync.WaitGroup\n\tfor _, nodeName := range nodeNames {\n\t\tfeedWg.Add(1)\n\t\tgo func(nodeName string) {\n\t\t\tdefer feedWg.Done()\n\t\t\tif err := c.waitForNodeDeployTarget(ctx, nodeName, completed); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tfinish(nodeName, err)\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tselect {\n\t\t\tcase input <- nodeName:\n\t\t\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n\t\t\t\tfinish(nodeName, ctx.Err())\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}(nodeName)\n\t}\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tfeedWg.Wait()\n\t\tclose(input)\n\t}()\n\n\tvar errs []error\n\tfor range nodeNames {\n\t\tif err := <-errCh; err != nil {\n\t\t\terrs = append(errs, err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif len(errs) > 0 {\n\t\treturn errors.Join(errs...)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\ntype nodeDeployCompletion struct {\n\tdone chan struct{}\n\t// Closing done publishes err to dependent goroutines.\n\terr error\n}\n\nfunc (c *CLab) waitForNodeDeployTarget(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tname string,\n\tcompleted map[string]*nodeDeployCompletion,\n) error {\n\tif err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\ttarget := networkModeContainerTarget(c.Nodes[name].Config().NetworkMode)\n\tif target == \"\" {\n\t\ttarget = c.Nodes[name].Config().Labels[clabnodestailscale.ParentLabel]\n\t}\n\tif completion, selected := completed[target]; selected {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase <-completion.done:\n\t\t\tif completion.err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\n\t\t\t\t\t\"node %q depends on failed node %q: %w\",\n\t\t\t\t\tname,\n\t\t\t\t\ttarget,\n\t\t\t\t\tcompletion.err,\n\t\t\t\t)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n\t\t\treturn ctx.Err()\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn c.waitForApplyNetworkModeTarget(ctx, name)\n}\n\n// waitForApplyNetworkModeTarget waits until a node's network-mode:\n// container:<target> target is running, so apply's incremental deploy never\n// races a newly created or recreated target against its dependent. The\n// target may be a node in this same apply call (added or recreated\n// alongside the dependent) or, like fresh deploy, an external container not\n// managed by this topology.\nfunc (c *CLab) waitForApplyNetworkModeTarget(ctx context.Context, nodeName string) error {\n\tnode, exists := c.Nodes[nodeName]\n\tif !exists {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\ttarget := networkModeContainerTarget(node.Config().NetworkMode)\n\tif target == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\ttargetNode, internal := c.Nodes[target]\n\tif !internal {\n\t\treturn c.waitForExternalNodeDependencies(ctx, nodeName)\n\t}\n\n\truntime := c.globalRuntime()\n\tif runtime == nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"container runtime is not initialized\")\n\t}\n\n\treturn clabruntime.WaitForContainerRunning(ctx, runtime, targetNode.Config().LongName, nodeName)\n}\n\nfunc (c *CLab) deployNode(ctx context.Context, node clabnodes.Node) error {\n\tnodeName := node.GetShortName()\n\tif err := node.PreDeploy(ctx, &clabnodes.PreDeployParams{\n\t\tCert:         c.Cert,\n\t\tTopologyName: c.Config.Name,\n\t\tTopoPaths:    c.TopoPaths,\n\t\tSSHPubKeys:   c.SSHPubKeys,\n\t}); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"node %q pre-deploy: %w\", nodeName, err)\n\t}\n\n\tif err := node.Deploy(ctx, &clabnodes.DeployParams{Nodes: c.Nodes}); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"node %q deploy: %w\", nodeName, err)\n\t}\n\n\tif err := node.UpdateConfigWithRuntimeInfo(ctx); err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Errorf(\"failed to update node runtime information for node %s: %v\", nodeName, err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// DeployLinks deploys selected links through their endpoints and runs their post-deploy hooks.\nfunc (c *CLab) DeployLinks(ctx context.Context, links []clablinks.Link) error {\n\treturn c.deployLinks(ctx, links, nil)\n}\n\nfunc (c *CLab) deployLinks(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tlinks []clablinks.Link,\n\tadditionalNodeNames []string,\n) error {\n\ttouchedNodes := make(map[string]struct{}, len(additionalNodeNames))\n\tfor _, nodeName := range additionalNodeNames {\n\t\ttouchedNodes[nodeName] = struct{}{}\n\t}\n\tfor _, link := range links {\n\t\tlog.Info(\"Creating link\", \"link\", applyLinkName(link))\n\n\t\tfor _, ep := range clablinks.RuntimeEndpoints(link) {\n\t\t\tif err := ep.Deploy(ctx); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed deploying link %s: %w\", applyLinkName(link), err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\ttouchedNodes[ep.GetNode().GetShortName()] = struct{}{}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif err := link.PostDeploy(ctx); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed post-deploying link %s: %w\", applyLinkName(link), err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, nodeName := range sortedStringSet(touchedNodes) {\n\t\tnode, exists := c.Nodes[nodeName]\n\t\tif !exists {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := node.PostDeployEndpoints(ctx); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"node %q post-deploy links: %w\", nodeName, err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc (c *CLab) postDeployApplyNodes(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tnodeNames []string,\n\tskipPostDeploy bool,\n) error {\n\texecCollection := clabexec.NewExecCollection()\n\n\tfor _, nodeName := range nodeNames {\n\t\tnode := c.Nodes[nodeName]\n\n\t\tif !skipPostDeploy {\n\t\t\tif err := node.PostDeploy(\n\t\t\t\tctx,\n\t\t\t\t&clabnodes.PostDeployParams{Nodes: c.Nodes},\n\t\t\t); err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Errorf(\"failed to create hosts file: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tlog.Info(\"Adding SSH config for nodes\", \"path\", c.TopoPaths.SSHConfigPath())\n\tif err := c.addSSHConfig(); err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Errorf(\"failed to create ssh config file: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tc.writeState()\n\n\treturn containers, nil\n}\n\nfunc (c *CLab) writeState() {\n\tif err := c.WriteState(); err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Warnf(\"failed to write state file: %v\", err)\n\t}\n}\n\n// configureSnapshotRestore configures nodes for snapshot restoration.\n// It resolves snapshot files for each node and adds the necessary volume mounts\n// and environment variables to restore from snapshots.\nfunc (c *CLab) configureSnapshotRestore(options *DeployOptions) error {\n\t// Build restore map from per-node specifications\n\trestoreMap := make(map[string]string)\n\tfor _, mapping := range options.restoreNodeSnapshots {\n\t\tparts := strings.SplitN(mapping, \"=\", 2)\n\t\tif len(parts) != 2 {\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"invalid restore format: %s (expected: node=path)\", mapping)\n\t\t}\n\t\trestoreMap[parts[0]] = parts[1]\n\t}\n\n\t// Track statistics for logging\n\tvar restoredCount, freshCount int\n\n\t// Configure each node\n\tfor _, node := range c.Nodes {\n\t\tnodeName := node.Config().ShortName\n\n\t\t// Resolve snapshot path for this node\n\t\tsnapshotPath, shouldRestore := resolveNodeSnapshot(nodeName, restoreMap, options.restoreAll)\n\n\t\tif shouldRestore {\n\t\t\t// Validate snapshot file exists\n\t\t\tif _, err := os.Stat(snapshotPath); Reasoning
Interesting — this repo has an `apply` reconcile flow. So a legacy lab (deployed by older clab, no holder) will be reconciled by the new binary via `c.apply`. The apply plan compares the current topology state vs desired. For the srl node, the desired config now includes the holder. Would apply detect the node as "needs recreation" (since its runtime config differs — network-mode changed from bridge to container:holder)? If apply detects a diff, it would recreate the node — destroying the running legacy node's config (unless preserved). The comment in the PR says "keeps working ... until it is recreated" — implying reconcile may or may not recreate it. Let me check `apply` logic: `c.runtimeNodeGroups`, `needsInitialDeploy`, apply plan building — `plan.recreatedNodeSet` etc. Look at apply.go to see how it computes diffs (ComputeDiff on NodeConfig). The legacy node's stored config (in lab dir state) has NetworkMode="" and mgmt network attached; the new desired config would have... wait, actually for the *node* config, `n.Cfg` still has NetworkMode="" (containerConfig is only used at CreateContainer time). The stored/running config from old clab would be similar. Does ComputeDiff consider the holder? The node's config is unchanged. So apply would consider the node unchanged, not recreate it — it would just "start" it if stopped. That matches the PR: Start handles legacy.

But wait — there's a subtlety: after apply, if the legacy node is started via the new Start path (DefaultNode.Start), the holder container was never created. Then `GetNSPath` fallback handles ns path. But links: during apply, links to the legacy node — endpoints attach to the node's netns. Endpoints use the node's ns path (via GetNSPath fallback) — should work.

However: does apply recreate the node when it detects the holder missing? Let me check apply.go for how it determines recreation, and whether it consults the netns holder existence. If apply only diffs topology config, the legacy node stays. Good — that's the intent.

But here's a potential real bug: after the legacy node is started via `DefaultNode.Start`, `n.GetNSPath` falls back to the node's own ns path — good. But what about `Stop`? Stop stops the node (DefaultNode.Stop) and holder is NotFound → returns nil. Then Start again → legacy path again. OK.

And `Delete` — deletes both; holder DeleteContainer on NotFound container — check DefaultNode.Delete: does it handle NotFound gracefully? Let me check DefaultNode.Delete.
